# Kazehana-chō census

Generated from seed 7 and the town plan by `cargo run -p census --example build` (the data: `assets/town/census.json`; how the town is made up: `docs/population_plan.md`). Ages are on 1 April 2026.

## The town in numbers

- **1392** people live in town, in **498** households (2.80 a household), in **471** houses.
- **337** more are family who live away (the cities, university), on record as relatives.
- 65 and over: 29% (410); under 15: 18% (254).
